`def full_conv(p):` Use real FFT:
```
N=len(p)
size=1
while size < 2*N-1: size <<=1
F=np.fft.rfft(p, size)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F.conj(), size)[:2*N-1]
return c
```
Since p real nonnegative, F*F.conj() = |F|^2 real; use `F * F.conj()` or `F.real**2 - F.imag**2`. Use `np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F)**2, size)` (abs squared real) good. Small negative due FFT; clip.

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def conv_fft(p):
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F.conj(), size)[:2*n-1]
    # Clean tiny negatives
    # c[abs(c)<1e-15]=0
    return c

def max_conv_score_fft(p):
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    pnorm=p/s
    c=conv_fft(pnorm)
    return 2*len(p)*float(np.max(c))

def smooth_max_value(c, beta):
    # returns (sc_max, weights)
    cmax=np.max(c)
    if beta<=0: w=np.zeros_like(c); w[c==cmax]=1/... return cmax
    x=beta*(c-cmax)
    # for numerical stability, if beta*cmax too high, x near0 for peak
    sm=np.sum(np.exp(x))
    w=np.exp(x)/sm
    # smoothed value = (1/beta)*log(sm) (since subtract cmax => cmax + log/sm? Actually logsumexp beta c = beta cmax + log sum exp(beta(c-cmax)); (1/beta)log(...) = cmax + (1/beta)log(sm).)
    return cmax + np.log(sm)/beta, w

def grad_from_weights(w, p):
    # lengths: w 2n-1, p n
    r=np.correlate(w, p, mode='valid')
    return 2.0*r
```
Check `np.correlate(w,p,mode='valid')`: If w length 2n-1, p n. For n=1, w len1, valid len1. For n=2, w len3, p len2, valid len2. For w=[w0,w1,w2], p=[p0,p1]. Output [w0 p0 + w1 p1? np.correlate(a,v,valid) with a longer: c[0]=∑ a[n] v[n]? Need verify: For a length3, v length2, valid c[0]=a[0]*v[0]+a[1]*v[1]; c[1]=a[1]*v[0]+a[2]*v[1]. Our r_j=∑ w_{j+i} p_i. For j=0: w0p0+w1p1 yes. j=1: w1p0+w2p1. Good.
Gradient =2r.

    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(n)+1)+1-n >0)[0][-1]? Standard:
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond=u > (cssv[?]-1)/? Need implement correct.

Standard Du & Foster:
```
def project_simplex(v, s=1):
    n=v.shape[0]
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)) + 1 - cssv > 0)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

Smooth full PGD:
```
def refine_pgd(p, budget_s, beta=None, step=None):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return p, np.inf
    p=p/s
    n=len(p)
    if beta is None: beta=20.0*n
    step = 0.1 if step is None else step
    best=p; best_score=2*n*np.max(conv_fft(best))
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    n_iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=conv_fft(p); smax,w=smooth_max_value(c,beta)
       G=grad_from_weights(w,p)
       # optional normalize G by max abs to stable
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(G))
       if not finite or gmax<=0: break
       y = p - step*(G/gmax)  # use normalized gradient step; adjust effective step
       q=project_simplex(y)
       # try line search: compute score_candidate maybe cmax or smooth
       if projection all zero? fallback
       sc = 2*n*np.max(conv_fft(q))
       if sc < best_score:
           best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; step *=1.1
       else:
           # try smaller
           for mult in [0.5,0.25,0.1]:
             q2=project_simplex(p - step*mult*(G/gmax)); sc2=...
             if sc2 < best_score: accept; step*=0.9? break
           else step*=0.5
       if time... maybe break if step too small
    return best,best_score
```

Chebyshev polynomial computation:
```
def cheb_values(x, D):
    # x in [-1,1]
    T = np.empty((x.size,D))
    T[:,0]=1
    T[:,1]=x
    for d in range(2,D): T[:,d]=2*x*T[:,d-1]-T[:,d-2]
    return T[:,1:] # omit T0 or include? Include T0 maybe fix.
```
We can include T0 column but fix c[0]=0? If using L-BFGS, set constant term not optimized? We'll create coefficients length D for d=1..D. No T0. Good.

Profile function:
```
def profile_from_coeff(c, basis):
    L = basis @ c
    L = L - np.max(L)
    p=np.exp(L)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p uniform
    return p/s
```

Top-level functions:
```
def model_smooth_obj(c, B, beta):
    p = profile_from_coeff(c, B)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    smax, w = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    return smax
def model_grad(c, B, beta):
    p = profile_from_coeff(c, B)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    smax, w = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    G = grad_from_weights(w,p)
    dL = p*(G - float(np.dot(p,G)))
    grad = B.T.dot(dL)
    return grad
```

obj += mu*np.dot(c,c); grad += 2*mu*c
```

def build_conv_matrix(f, active_mask=None):
   n=len(f); L=2*n-1
   if active_mask is None or True: rows all
   A = np.empty((m,n), dtype=float)
   for d in range(n):
      coeff=f[d]
      cols=np.arange(n)
      ks=d+cols # 0..d+n-1
      if mask is None: s=ks<d? no
      valid = (ks>=0)&(ks<L)
      if mask is not None: valid &= mask[ks]
      A[np.nonzero(valid)[0], cols[valid]] = coeff
   return A
```
For full m=L, this fills triangular banded. Use loops n, each vector length n; O(n^2). For n=1024, okay. Use dense.

Let's define:
```
def make_cheb_basis(N, D):
    x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, N)  # maybe y=2i/(N-1)-1
    B = np.empty((N,D), dtype=float)
    if D>0: B[:,0]=x
    for d in range(2,D+1): B[:,d]=2*x*B[:,d-1]-B[:,d-2]
    return B
```
Using N points from -1 to1 inclusive. For convolution of discrete positions, endpoints included. Good.

For infinite continuous, endpoints don't matter; discrete including endpoint can create higher values that affect convolution edges. def profile_from_coeff(c, B):
    L = B.dot(c)
    var = np.max(L)
    if not finite: uniform
    e = np.exp(L - var)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(N,1/N) inferred? Need N = B.shape[0]
    return e/s
```

def model_gradient(c,B,beta,mu):
    p=...

    smax,w=smooth_max_value(cconv,beta)
    G=conv_grad_from_weights(w,p)
    meanG=float(np.dot(p,G))
    dL=p*(G-meanG)

Local_descent algorithm robust:
```
def local_descent(p, budget_s=120.0, beta_factor=20.0):
    deadline=...
    p=normalize
    n=len(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=quick_score(p)
    beta=beta_factor*n
    step=1.0
    fail=0
    while time<deadline:
        c=conv_fft(p)
        M=c.max()
        if M? score=2nM
        w=softmax(beta*(c-M)); w/=sum
        G=2*correlate(w,p,valid)
        # Try several step multipliers (0.2,0.5,1,2?) raw
        improved=False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0):
            y=p - mult*step*G
            q=project_simplex(y)
            # avoid unchanged
            if np.allclose(q,best): continue
            sc=quick_score(q)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                p=q; best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True; fail=0
                if mult<1.0: step=max(0.01, step*1.1)
                break
        if not improved:
            fail +=1
            step *= 0.5
            if fail>20 or step<1e-4:
                # vary beta, random jump
                beta *= 1.5 if beta < 1000*n else 20*n
                if beta == 20*n: fail=0
                # small random smoothing salt
                noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)); q=p*noise; q=normalize; if better accept
                if fail>50: break
    return best,best_score
```
But using raw G with step=1 might be too aggressive for some; line search includes 0.25. Good.

Project_simplex implementation must be robust. Let's test with v length n. Standard:
```
def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = (cssv - 1.0)/k  # theta candidates? Actually for rho: u[rho] > (cssv[rho]-s)/rho.
    rho = np.nonzero(u > (cssv - s)/k)[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - s)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
Wait condition uses 1-indexed rho: find largest ρ such that u_ρ - (Σ_{i≤ρ}u_i - s)/ρ >0. In 0-index rho_i, condition u[i] > (cssv[i]-s)/(i+1). So:
```
rho = np.nonzero(u - (cssv - s)/(np.arange(1,n+1)) > 0)[0]
if len(rho)==0: rho=0
theta = (cssv[rho] - s)/(rho+1)
return np.maximum(v - theta,0)
```
This works. Ensure if s small? p sum1. If v has nan? no.

Can use `scipy.optimize.project`? no.

Use `np.fft.irfft(F * F.conj(), size)` also. `np.abs(F)**2` is real but may have tiny negative? no. Use that.

Let's write helper functions in detail.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import scipy.optimize as optimize_mod

def _fft_size(n):
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    return size

def conv_fft(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    size=_fft_size(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F)**2, size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negatives
    # c[c<0 & > -1e-15]=0 maybe; for max, negative not matter unless all? 
    return c

def quick_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return float('inf')
    p=p/s
    c=conv_fft(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    return float(2*p.size*np.max(c))
```
Since p nonnegative, max c positive. If FFT small negative, max unaffected.

`smooth_max_value`:
```
def smooth_max_value(c, beta):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0:
       idx=np.where(c==cmax)[0]
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx); return cmax,w
    x=beta*(c-cmax)
    # x max 0
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       idx=np.where(c==cmax)[0]; w=...
       return cmax,w
    w=ex/s
    smax=cmax + float(np.log(s))/beta
    return smax,w
```
If beta*cmax large? x subtract cmax so max 0; log(s)/beta positive small. Good.

Gradient:
```
def gradient_from_weights(w,p):
    n=p.size
    size=_fft_size(n)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp.conj(), size)[:n]
    # Clean tiny imaginary? irfft real. Due to round maybe negative.
    return g
```
But if we already have Fp from `conv_fft`, we recompute. In local descent, combine:
```
def conv_and_gradient(p,w):
    n=p.size; size=_fft_size(n); Fp=rfft(p,size); c=irfft(abs(Fp)^2,size)[:2n-1]; Fw=rfft(w,size); g=2*irfft(Fw*Fp.conj(),size)[:n]; return c,g
```
Use in local_descent.

Project simplex:
```
def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(cssv - s)/k
    # condition u > theta
    ok = u > theta
    if not np.any(ok):
        return np.full(n, s/n)
    rho=np.nonzero(ok)[0][-1]
    theta=theta[rho]
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
Test: v=[1,0,0], s=1. u=[1,0,0], cssv=[1,1,1], theta=[0,0.5,0.333]; ok: [T,F,F], rho0 theta0=0 => [1,0,0] good. v=[2,0,0], u=[2,0,0], cssv=[2,2,2], theta=[1,1,0.667], ok [1>1? False,0>1 F], none -> returns uniform [0.5? wait s/n=0.5]? But true projection of [2,0,0] onto simplex sum1 is [1,0,0]? Let's check condition: rho=0: u0 - (cssv0-s)/1 =2-(2-1)=1>0, so ok T. Our condition u > (cssv-s)/k: 2>1 true. I used theta=(cssv-s)/k; for v=[2], theta0=1, ok 2>1. Good. For v=[1,0,0], theta0=0, 1>0 true. If v=[0.5,0.5], u=[0.5,0.5], cssv=[0.5,1], theta=[-0.5,0], ok 0.5>-0.5 T, 0.5>0 T, rho1 theta0 => [0.5,0.5] good. For v=[-1,-1], s=1: u=[-1,-1], cssv=[-1,-2], theta=[-2,-1.5], ok T,T, rho1 theta=-1.5 => v-theta=[0.5,0.5] good. If no ok, e.g. v all? Shouldn't happen for s>0. Good.

`normalize_nonnegative(p)`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,None)
    if not finite: p=ones
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(p); s=p.sum()
    return p/s
```

`profile_from_coeff`:
```
def profile_from_coeff(c, B):
    n=B.shape[0]
    L=B.dot(c)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(L)): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    m=np.max(L)
    e=np.exp(L-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return full
    return e/s
```

`make_cheb_basis`:
```
def make_cheb_basis(n,D):
    x=np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,n)
    B=np.empty((n,D),float)
    if D>0: B[:,0]=x
    for d in range(1,D): # d 1-index? Need fill T_{d+1}
       if d==0: continue
    # Let's use for idx in range(1,D): T[idx]=2x*T[idx-1]-T[idx-2]
    for idx in range(2,D):
       B[:,idx] = 2.0*x*B[:,idx-1] - B[:,idx-2]
    return B
```

Param objective functions with args. Because `args` can contain numpy B, okay.
```
def model_objective(c,B,beta,scale,mu):
    p=profile_from_coeff(c,B)
    cconv=conv_fft(p)
    smax,w=smooth_max_value(cconv,beta)
    val=smax
    if mu>0: val += mu*float(np.dot(c,c))
    return float(scale*val)
def model_gradient(c,B,beta,scale,mu):
    p=profile_from_coeff(c,B)
    cconv=conv_fft(p)
    smax,w=smooth_max_value(cconv,beta)
    G=gradient_from_weights(w,p)
    meanG=float(np.dot(p,G))
    dL=p*(G-meanG)
    grad=B.T.dot(dL)
    if mu>0: grad += 2*mu*c
    return scale*grad
```
Need scale maybe n*n. For N=2048, scale=4e6, smax 2e-4 => objective 800. gradient dL~? p*(G-mean), p~5e-4, G~? weights sum1, correlate p maybe ~1/n? ~5e-4, product ~2e-7, B.T sum N terms ~1? Actually B columns O(1), sum ~N*2e-7=1e-3? scale 4e6 => 4000. Good.

If L-BFGS with large objective, okay. `ftol` absolute 1e-8 maybe too small for objective 800; use relative automatically? L-BFGS uses `ftol` absolute change in f. Since objective scaled ~100, `ftol=1e-8` means needs change 1e-8, maybe okay. Could set `ftol=1e-10`. Smoothing.

Full softmax L-BFGS functions:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    m=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
def full_objective(z,beta,scale,mu):
    p=softmax_from_z(z)
    cconv=conv_fft(p)
    smax,w=smooth_max_value(...)
    val=smax
    if mu>0:
       zc=z-z.mean(); val += mu*float(np.dot(zc,zc))
    return float(scale*val)
def full_gradient(z,beta,scale,mu):
    p=softmax_from_z(z)
    ...
    dL=p*(G-meanG)
    if mu>0: dL += 2*mu*(z-z.mean())
    return scale*dL
```

def refine_softmax_lbgfs(p0, time_budget_s, beta=None, maxiter=None):
   n=p0.size
   p0=normalize_nonnegative(p0)
   z=np.log(p0+1e-300)
   z -= np.mean(z)
   if maxiter is None: maxiter=300
   if beta is None: beta=30.0*n
   bounds=[(-50.0,50.0)]*n
   options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxls':20, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxfun': maxiter*10}
   try: res=optimize_mod.minimize(full_objective, z, jac=full_gradient, args=(beta, float(n*n), 0.0), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
   except Exception: return p0
   p=softmax_from_z(res.x); return normalize_nonnegative(p)
```

Implement `local_descent(p, budget_s, beta_factor=30.0)`.
Use FFT gradient. It will update in-place until time. Return best p and score. It should not use official. It can use `quick_score` each candidate. Since quick_score uses FFT, fine. Need ensure no too many slow; we can line search with up to 3 candidates. We'll use step sizes [0.5,1.0,2.0] raw with step variable? Let's implement robust:
```
def local_descent(p, budget_s, beta_factor=30.0):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=quick_score(p)
    beta=beta_factor*n
    step=1.0
    fail=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=conv_fft(p)
        _,w=smooth_max_value(c,beta)
        c2,g=conv_and_gradient(p,w) # reuse? conv_fft already, but okay
        # Actually c from first conv; gradient needs Fp recompute. We can just call gradient_from_weights.
        if not finite g: break
        improved=False
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0):
            y=p - step*mult*g
            q=project_simplex(y)
            if np.allclose(q,p,rtol=0,atol=1e-15): continue
            sc=quick_score(q)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                p=q; best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True; step=min(10.0, step*1.05); fail=0; break
        if not improved:
            fail+=1
            step*=0.5
            if fail>10:
                beta = min(200.0*n, beta*1.5)
                # random jitter
                nz=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.02,n))
                q=normalize_nonnegative(p*nz)
                sc=quick_score(q)
                if sc < best_score:
                   p=q; best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; fail=0
                else:
                   beta=30.0*n; step=1.0
                   fail= (fail-10)
            if fail>60 or step<1e-5:
                break
    return best,best_score
```
Potential issue: raw gradient g may have very small magnitude; step*mult may be too small. But normalized? We can scale g by `(n)`? Let's estimate p~1/n, weights w for active peak maybe w concentrated O(1) on few k. g_j =2∑ w_k p_{k-j}. If one peak k, g_j=2p_j ~2/n. So g ~1/n. p ~1/n. Projection subtract step*g: if step=1, changes p by 1/n, relative O(1). Good. If weights spread over many k, g could be O(1) if w not concentrated? w sum1; correlate with p sum could be up to sum p =1? Actually if w heavily on all k? w sum1, p positive, g_j maybe up to 2∑ w_k p... ≤2*max p? Wait sum over k w_k p_{k-j}; for fixed j, at most one k for each i? If w has mass over many k, sum w_k ≤1; p≤O(1) if p peaked? For smooth p ~1/n, g~1/n. If p peaked, p max could be large (e.g., 1), then g can be O(1); step=1 may be too large. But objective with peaked p score high; projection will clip. Accept.

Maybe use `step=0.1` initially to be safe. We can line search includes 0.25. If no improvement, reduce step. But if step too small, slow. Use `step=0.5` initial.

We can normalize g by its L∞ and use step in positions? If gmax~1/n, p~1/n; step=1.0 okay. Use `step = 0.5` raw.

Local_descent can be computationally heavy if quick_score after each mult (FFT). We limit budget.

def consider_profile(p, best_seq, best_official, best_local, best_local_p, use_official=True):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    local=quick_score(p)
    if finite and local < best_local:
       best_local=local; best_local_p=p.copy()
    if use_official:
       off=eval_official(p)
       if finite and off < best_official:
          best_seq=p.copy(); best_official=off
    return best_seq,best_official,best_local,best_local_p
```

def run_param_search(N,D,end_time,rng,best_c,best_c_score,max_attempts, beta_factors, maxiter):

`refine_softmax_lbgfs` top-level:
```
def refine_softmax_lbgfs(p0, maxiter=200, beta_factor=30.0):
    p0=normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    n=p0.size
    z=np.log(p0 + 1e-300)
    z -= np.mean(z)
    z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
    beta=beta_factor*n
    scale=float(n*n)
    bounds=[(-50.0,50.0)]*n
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxls': 20, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-7, 'maxfun': int(maxiter*20)}
    try:
       res=sp_opt.minimize(full_objective, z, jac=full_gradient, args=(beta,scale,0.0), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
       p=softmax_from_z(res.x)
    except Exception:
       p=p0
    return normalize_nonnegative(p)
```
Potential memory: bounds list with 4096 tuples; okay. Each call creates.

`conv_and_gradient` maybe useful for local_descent to avoid recompute Fp. Implement:
```
def conv_and_gradient(p,w):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size; size=fft_size(n)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(np.abs(Fp)**2, size)[:2*n-1]
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp.conj(), size)[:n]
    return c,g
```
In local_descent, we call c from conv_fft? Use this once per iteration. It computes c and g. Good.

```
def run_param_search(N, D, end_time, rng, best_c, best_c_score, max_attempts, beta_factors, maxiter):
    if sp_opt is None: return best_c,best_c_score
    B=make_cheb_basis(N,D)
    scale=float(N*N)
    beta_list=[bf*N for bf in beta_factors]
    attempts=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time and attempts < max_attempts:
        c0=make_initial_coeff(rng,D,attempts,best_c)
        beta=beta_list[attempts % len(beta_list)]
        try:
           res=sp_opt.minimize(model_objective, c0, jac=model_gradient, args=(B,beta,scale,1e-10), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*30,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':10})
           if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
               p=profile_from_coeff(res.x,B)
               s=quick_score(p)
               if s < best_c_score:
                   best_c=res.x; best_c_score=s
                   print(...)
        except Exception:
           pass
        attempts += 1
    return best_c,best_c_score
```

`make_initial_coeff` uses `np.arange`? no.

Let's define:
```
def make_initial_coeff(rng,D,attempt,best_c):
    c=np.zeros(D)
    if D==0: return c
    mod=attempt % 7
    if mod==0:
        return c
    if mod==1:
        a=float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0))
        c[0]=0.5*a
        c[1]=float(rng.normal(0,0.3)) if D>1 else 0
        return c
    if mod==2 and best_c is not None:
        return best_c + rng.normal(0,0.4,D)
    if mod==3:
        scale=float(rng.uniform(0.5,4.0))
        return rng.normal(0.0,scale,D)
    if mod==4:
        c[0]=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,3.0))
        if D>1: c[1]=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))
        if D>2: c[2]=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0))
        return c
    if mod==5:
        scale=float(rng.uniform(1.0,8.0))
        return rng.normal(0.0,scale,D)
    # mod 6: ramp + higher noise
    c[0]=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0))
    c += rng.normal(0,0.5,D)
    return c
```
This is top-level.

Maybe best_c from geometric a=1 has c[0]=0.5. Good.

```
def geometric_profile(n,a):
 x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
 if n>1: x=x/float(n-1)
 else: x=np.array([0.0])
 p=np.exp(a*x)
 return normalize_nonnegative(p)
```

Top-level code:
```

`fft_size(n)`:
```
def fft_size(n):
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size < need:
        size <<=1
    return size
```

`conv_fft`: if p.size 0 return zeros. Use `np.float64`.
```
def conv_fft(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    size=fft_size(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F)**2, size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    return c
```

`quick_score`:
```
def quick_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return inf
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return inf
    p=p/s
    c=conv_fft(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    return float(2.0*p.size*float(np.max(c)))
```

`normalize_nonnegative` as above.

`smooth_max_value`:
```
def smooth_max_value(c,beta):
   c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if c.size==0: return 0.0, np.array([])
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if beta <= 0:
      w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.where(c==cmax)[0]; if idx.size: w[idx]=1/idx.size; else w[0]=1
      return cmax,w
   x=beta*(c-cmax)
   ex=np.exp(x)
   s=float(np.sum(ex))
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
      w=zeros; idx peaks; w[idx]=1/len; return cmax,w
   w=ex/s
   val=cmax + float(np.log(s))/beta
   return val,w
```
If cmax negative? impossible, but okay.

`gradient_from_weights(w,p)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(w,p):
    n=p.size; size=fft_size(n)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp.conj(), size)[:n]
    return g
```

`project_simplex`: use float.

`profile_from_coeff`, `make_cheb_basis`.

`conv_and_gradient` for local:
```
def conv_and_gradient(p,w):
   n=p.size; size=fft_size(n); Fp=rfft(p,size); c=irfft(abs(Fp)**2,size)[:2*n-1]; Fw=rfft(w,size); g=2*irfft(Fw*Fp.conj(),size)[:n]; return c,g
```

`local_descent` uses `conv_and_gradient`. It should normalize p at start, and after project. If projection returns sum slightly off? project_simplex sum exactly? Gaussian round maybe 1. We can renormalize after project: `q=q/q.sum() if sum>0`. But project already. For safety.

Implement `local_descent`:
```
def local_descent(p, budget_s, beta_factor=30.0):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=quick_score(p)
    if budget_s <=0: return best,best_score
    beta=beta_factor*n
    step=0.5
    fail=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c,w=smooth_max_value(conv_fft(p), beta)? Need also gradient. Use c,w then gradient_from_weights (recomputes Fp). To save, use conv_fft once and gradient uses Fp again; okay.
        c=conv_fft(p)
        _,w=smooth_max_value(c,beta)
        g=gradient_from_weights(w,p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
        improved=False
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,2.0):
            y=p - step*mult*g
            q=project_simplex(y)
            s=float(np.sum(q))
            if s>0: q=q/s
            if np.allclose(q,p,rtol=0.0,atol=1e-16): continue
            sc=quick_score(q)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; p=q; improved=True; step=min(5.0, step*1.1); fail=0; break
        if not improved:
            fail+=1
            step*=0.5
            if fail > 8:
                beta = min(200.0*n, beta*1.5)
                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n))
                q=normalize_nonnegative(p*noise)
                sc=quick_score(q)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; p=q; fail=0
                else:
                    beta=beta_factor*n; step=0.5; fail=0
            if fail > 60 or step < 1e-6:
                break
    return best,best_score
```
`np.allclose` with large arrays each candidate 5 times => overhead. We can skip, just evaluate; if q almost same, quick_score same. Remove allclose for speed. Or check `if np.max(np.abs(q-p)) < 1e-15: continue` maybe. Not necessary.

Maybe raw gradient too strong for first mult 1.0; but line search. Good.

`softmax_from_z`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size==0: return np.array([])
   m=float(np.max(z))
   e=np.exp(z-m)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if not finite or s<=0: return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
   return e/s
```

Now, correctness of FFT gradient orientation one more time. `Fw * Fp.conj()` -> inverse gives cross-correlation `∑ w_{n} p_{n+k}`? Let's verify with simple via reasoning. Circular cross-correlation formula: `ifft(FFT(a) * conj(FFT(b)))_k = ∑_n a_{n+k} b_n` (mod N). We want r_j=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} w_{j+i} p_i. Set len w W=2n-1, p length n padded to size S. For k=j, sum over i=0..n-1 a_{i+j} b_i. Terms where i+j up to n-1+n-1=2n-2<W, no wrap if S≥2n-1. Good. So r[j] correct.

Check gradient for c_k full convolution: If weights w_k, derivative wrt p_i? We used r_i=∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. Output r via formula with lag i: ∑_j w_{j+i} p_j where j= p index. Let i=j? This is ∑_k w_{k-i} p_{k-i}? Let rename: r_i=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{j+i} p_j =∑_{k} w_{k-i} p_{k-i}. But earlier needed ∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. Off by sign? Let's set i (coordinate). Need r_i=∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i}, valid 0≤k-i<n. Let j=k-i => k=j+i. Then sum_j w_{j+i} p_j. Yes matches formula. Good. For k=0 weight w0, r_i=∑_j w_{j+i}p_j = w_i p_0 (since w only 0, j+i=0 -> j=-i no? Wait for i=0: j+0=0 ->j=0 p0. Good. For i=1: j+1=0 none. So r=[p0,0]. Good. For w1, r_0: j+1=1 ->j=1? p1; yes. r_1: j+1=1 ->j=0 p0. Good. So correct.

`evaluate_one` function:
```
def evaluate_one(seq):
    try:
        arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size==0: return float('inf')
        vals=[float(x) for x in arr]
        out=evaluate_sequence(vals)
        return float(out)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
```

Let's double-check `full_gradient` relation with penalty. If `mu` added to `zc`, scaling by `scale`; good.

`full_objective`: If z all equal (flat), p uniform, smax; penalty zero. Gradient dL? For uniform gradient maybe not zero? If p uniform and weights symmetric, G? Let's compute dL = p*(G-meanG). If p uniform and G not constant, dL nonzero, so moves. If G constant, dL zero. Fine.

One minor bug: `smooth_max_value` for beta <= 0: If idx size zero (should not), set w[0]=1. okay.

Now, final code. Need set docstring top summarizing algorithm. Include inside code.

Let's produce final. But before final, let's mentally run small with N=1024; no obvious errors.

Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F)**2, size)` expects `np.abs(F)**2` real; it will treat as complex with 0 imaginary. Good.

Potential issue: In `gradient_from_weights`, size from fft_size(n) = next power two >=2n-1. For `w` length 2n-1, if size == 2n-1 exact? It uses power of two; since 2n-1 not power of two, size >. Good.

Potential issue: The correlation formula using circular correlation with padded arrays of length size. For index lag j up to n-1, terms use a index i+j where i up to n-1, max 2n-2. size >2n-1, no wrap. b index i padded zeros beyond n-1. Good.

Let's test with n=2, size=4. w=[w0,w1,w2,0], p=[p0,p1,0,0]. ifft(FFT(w)*conj(FFT(p))) at k=0: w0p0+w1p1? formula maybe includes p2? zero. k=1: w1p0+w2p1. Good.

Now, final code with prints. Ensure not too many prints from run_param_search if many improvements. Cap by printing every improvement; likely few.

Let's now craft final answer.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
by combining:
  - Chebyshev-log-density low-dimensional optimization,
  - full discrete softmax L-BFGS refinement,
  - a projected smoothed-subgradient local descent,
  - adaptive random restarts/geometric initializers.
The final answer is a long (typically 4096-point) sequence scaled to sum 100
to avoid underflow in the exact evaluator while preserving scale invariance.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sp_opt
except Exception:
    sp_opt = None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fft_size(n):
    size = 1
    need = 2 * n - 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    return size


def conv_fft(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = fft_size(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F) ** 2, size)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def conv_and_gradient(p, w):
    """Return (p*p, grad) where grad is 2*correlate(w,p) valid part."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    size = fft_size(n)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(Fp) ** 2, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp.conj(), size)[:n]
    return c, g


def gradient_from_weights(w, p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    size = fft_size(n)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    return 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp.conj(), size)[:n]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic sequence utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_nonnegative(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        p = np.ones_like(p)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def quick_score(p):
    """FFT-based exact-scale score: 2n max(p*p) after normalizing sum to 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    c = conv_fft(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * p.size * float(np.max(c)))


def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    """Euclidean projection onto the simplex sum(v)=s, v>=0."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(n, s / n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    k = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cssv - float(s)) / k
    ok = u > theta
    if not np.any(ok):
        return np.full(n, s / n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(ok)[0][-1])
    theta = theta[rho]
    q = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    qs = float(np.sum(q))
    if qs > 0.0 and np.isfinite(qs):
        q = q / qs
    return q


def smooth_max_value(c, beta):
    """
    Smooth maximum of c.
    Returns the smooth value and normalized softmax weights on probabilities.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = np.where(c == cmax)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            w[0] = 1.0
        else:
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return cmax, w
    x = beta * (c - cmax)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = np.where(c == cmax)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            w[0] = 1.0
        else:
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return cmax, w
    w = ex / s
    val = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    return val, w


def evaluate_one(seq):
    """Safe wrapper around the externally provided evaluate_sequence."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
        out = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        return float(out)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Chebyshev-log-density model
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_cheb_basis(n, D):
    """Basis [T_1(x), T_2(x), ..., T_D(x)] on x in [-1,1]."""
    x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)
    if D > 0:
        B[:, 0] = x
    for i in range(2, D):
        B[:, i] = 2.0 * x * B[:, i - 1] - B[:, i - 2]
    return B


def profile_from_coeff(c, B):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = B.shape[0]
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    L = B.dot(c)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(L)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(L))
    e = np.exp(L - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def model_objective(c, B, beta, scale, mu):
    p = profile_from_coeff(c, B)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    smax, _ = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    val = smax
    if mu > 0.0:
        val += mu * float(np.dot(c, c))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return float(scale * val)


def model_gradient(c, B, beta, scale, mu):
    p = profile_from_coeff(c, B)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    _, w = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    G = gradient_from_weights(w, p)
    meanG = float(np.dot(p, G))
    dL = p * (G - meanG)
    grad = B.T.dot(dL)
    if mu > 0.0:
        grad = grad + 2.0 * mu * c
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(scale) * grad


def make_initial_coeff(rng, D, attempt, best_c):
    c = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    if D == 0:
        return c
    mod = attempt % 7

    if mod == 0:
        return c

    if mod == 1:
        # Geometric / ramp-like start: exp(a*x) ~ exp((a/2)*T1(2x-1)).
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
        c[0] = 0.5 * a
        if D > 1:
            c[1] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3))
        return c

    if mod == 2 and best_c is not None:
        return best_c + rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, D)

    if mod == 3:
        scale = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        return rng.normal(0.0, scale, D).astype(np.float64)

    if mod == 4:
        c[0] = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 3.0))
        if D > 1:
            c[1] = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        if D > 2:
            c[2] = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        return c

    if mod == 5:
        scale = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 8.0))
        return rng.normal(0.0, scale, D).astype(np.float64)

    c[0] = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
    return c + rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, D).astype(np.float64)


def run_param_search(N, D, end_time, rng, best_c, best_c_score, max_attempts,
                     beta_factors, maxiter):
    """Run many low-dimensional L-BFGS restarts on Chebyshev log-density model."""
    if sp_opt is None or D <= 0 or N <= 0:
        return best_c, best_c_score

    B = make_cheb_basis(N, D)
    scale = float(N * N)
    beta_list = [float(bf) * float(N) for bf in beta_factors]
    attempts = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time and attempts < max_attempts:
        c0 = make_initial_coeff(rng, D, attempts, best_c)
        beta = beta_list[attempts % len(beta_list)]
        mu = 1e-12
        try:
            res = sp_opt.minimize(
                model_objective,
                c0,
                jac=model_gradient,
                args=(B, beta, scale, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(max(20, 30 * maxiter)),
                    "ftol": 1e-10,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "maxls": 10,
                },
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                p = profile_from_coeff(res.x, B)
                s = quick_score(p)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_c_score:
                    best_c = np.array(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    best_c_score = float(s)
                    print("param N=%d  local-score=%.7f  attempts=%d" %
                          (N, best_c_score, attempts + 1), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
        attempts += 1

    return best_c, best_c_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full discrete softmax refinement
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def full_objective(z, beta, scale, mu):
    p = softmax_from_z(z)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    smax, _ = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    val = smax
    if mu > 0.0:
        zc = z - float(np.mean(z))
        val += mu * float(np.dot(zc, zc))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return float(scale * val)


def full_gradient(z, beta, scale, mu):
    p = softmax_from_z(z)
    cconv = conv_fft(p)
    _, w = smooth_max_value(cconv, beta)
    G = gradient_from_weights(w, p)
    meanG = float(np.dot(p, G))
    grad = p * (G - meanG)
    if mu > 0.0:
        grad = grad + 2.0 * mu * (z - float(np.mean(z)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(scale) * grad


def refine_softmax_lbgfs(p0, maxiter=150, beta_factor=30.0):
    """Refine a non-negative profile directly in log-space with L-BFGS."""
    p0 = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    n = p0.size
    if sp_opt is None or n <= 0:
        return p0
    z = np.log(p0 + 1e-300)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0).astype(np.float64)
    beta = float(beta_factor) * float(n)
    scale = float(n * n)
    mu = 1e-12
    try:
        res = sp_opt.minimize(
            full_objective,
            z,
            jac=full_gradient,
            args=(beta, scale, mu),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(max(50, 20 * maxiter)),
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            p = softmax_from_z(res.x)
            return normalize_nonnegative(p)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return p0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Projected sequential local descent
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def local_descent(p, budget_s, beta_factor=30.0):
    """
    Projected subgradient/softmax enhancement on the simplex.
    Fast and useful as a final polish.
    """
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = quick_score(p)
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    beta = float(beta_factor) * float(n)
    step = 0.5
    fail = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = conv_fft(p)
        _, w = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
        g = gradient_from_weights(w, p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 2.0):
            y = p - step * mult * g
            q = project_simplex(y, 1.0)
            qs = float(np.sum(q))
            if qs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(qs):
                continue
            q = q / qs
            sc = quick_score(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best = q.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                p = q
                improved = True
                step = min(5.0, step * 1.1)
                fail = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            fail += 1
            step *= 0.5
            if fail > 8:
                beta = min(200.0 * float(n), beta * 1.5)
                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n))
                q = normalize_nonnegative(p * noise)
                sc = quick_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = q.copy()
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    p = q
                    fail = 0
                else:
                    beta = float(beta_factor) * float(n)
                    step = 0.5
                    fail = 0
            if fail > 60 or step < 1e-6:
                break

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Simple robust parametrizations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def geometric_profile(n, a):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = x / float(n - 1)
    else:
        x = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.exp(a * x)
    return normalize_nonnegative(p)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search function
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Return a non-negative sequence intended to minimize the exact evaluator.
    The function uses the available CPU time to improve a smooth asymmetric
    density and refines it directly in the discrete space.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + max(90.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed % (2**32 - 1)))

    # Choose final length. 4096 is a good compromise: strong asymptotics and
    # still cheap for the final exact full convolution.
    if budget >= 1200:
        N_final = 4096
    elif budget >= 500:
        N_final = 2048
    else:
        N_final = 1024

    best_seq = None
    best_official = float("inf")

    # Final-length best local candidate.
    final_p = np.ones(N_final, dtype=np.float64) / float(N_final)
    final_local = quick_score(final_p)

    # Baseline exact score.
    base_score = evaluate_one(final_p)
    if np.isfinite(base_score):
        best_official = float(base_score)
        best_seq = final_p.copy()
    print("baseline uniform N=%d exact=%.9f" % (N_final, best_official), flush=True)

    # Quick geometric grid at final length.
    for a in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 4.0):
        p = geometric_profile(N_final, float(a))
        s = quick_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < final_local:
            final_local = float(s)
            final_p = p.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stage 1: low-dimensional Chebyshev optimization, small N.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    D = 16
    N_model = 1024 if budget >= 1500 else 512

    c_best = make_cheb_basis(N_model, D).dot(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))
    c_best = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    c_best[0] = 0.5  # exp(x)-like start
    p_best_c = profile_from_coeff(c_best, make_cheb_basis(N_model, D))
    c_best_score = quick_score(p_best_c)

    stage1_budget = min(600.0, max(120.0, (deadline - start) * 0.30))
    end1 = start + stage1_budget
    c_best, c_best_score = run_param_search(
        N_model,
        D,
        end1,
        rng,
        c_best,
        c_best_score,
        max_attempts=100000,
        beta_factors=(5.0, 15.0, 40.0),
        maxiter=120,
    )
    print("after stage1 N_model=%d best-model-local=%.7f" % (N_model, c_best_score), flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stage 2: if time permits, repeat with a larger model length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 1000:
        N_model2 = 2048 if N_model <= 1024 else 2048
        stage2_budget = min(500.0, rem * 0.30)
        if stage2_budget > 80:
            end2 = now + stage2_budget
            c_best, c_best_score = run_param_search(
                N_model2,
                D,
                end2,
                rng,
                c_best,
                c_best_score,
                max_attempts=100000,
                beta_factors=(5.0, 15.0, 40.0),
                maxiter=80,
            )
            print("after stage2 N_model=%d best-model-local=%.7f" % (N_model2, c_best_score), flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build final candidate from best Chebyshev coefficients.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if c_best is not None and c_best.size == D:
        Bf = make_cheb_basis(N_final, D)
        p_from_c = profile_from_coeff(c_best, Bf)
        s = quick_score(p_from_c)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < final_local:
            final_local = float(s)
            final_p = p_from_c.copy()

    # Include the exact score of the current best final candidate.
    off = evaluate_one(final_p)
    if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
        best_official = float(off)
        best_seq = final_p.copy()
    print("final candidate before refinement exact=%.9f" % best_official, flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Full discrete softmax refinement.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now

    # First full refinement of the best profile.
    if rem > 500:
        cand = refine_softmax_lbgfs(final_p, maxiter=180, beta_factor=30.0)
        s = quick_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < final_local:
            final_local = float(s)
            final_p = cand.copy()
        off = evaluate_one(final_p)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
            best_official = float(off)
            best_seq = final_p.copy()
        print("after full L-BFGS exact=%.9f" % best_official, flush=True)

    # Second refinement from a noisy perturbation of the current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 300:
        noisy = normalize_nonnegative(final_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, final_p.size)))
        cand = refine_softmax_lbgfs(noisy, maxiter=110, beta_factor=50.0)
        s = quick_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < final_local:
            final_local = float(s)
            final_p = cand.copy()
        off = evaluate_one(final_p)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
            best_official = float(off)
            best_seq = final_p.copy()
        print("after noisy L-BFGS exact=%.9f" % best_official, flush=True)

    # Final projected local polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 120:
        polish_budget = max(20.0, min(180.0, rem - 45.0))
        cand, loc = local_descent(final_p, polish_budget, beta_factor=35.0)
        if np.isfinite(loc) and loc < final_local:
            final_local = float(loc)
            final_p = cand.copy()
        off = evaluate_one(final_p)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
            best_official = float(off)
            best_seq = final_p.copy()
        print("after local polish exact=%.9f" % best_official, flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Sanitize and return one of two safe scalings.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_seq is None:
        arr = final_p
    else:
        arr = best_seq
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(N_final, dtype=np.float64) / float(N_final)
        s = 1.0

    arr1 = arr / s                      # sum 1
    arr100 = np.clip(arr1 * 100.0, 0.0, 1000.0)  # sum about 100, better rounding

    best_final = arr100
    best_final_score = float("inf")
    for cand in (arr1, arr100):
        sc = evaluate_one(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_final_score:
            best_final_score = float(sc)
            best_final = cand

    if np.isfinite(best_official) and best_official < best_final_score:
        # Prefer a previously exact-scored profile if scaling/rounding
        # unexpectedly made the sanitized candidate worse.
        best_final = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        best_final = np.clip(best_final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        fs = float(np.sum(best_final))
        if fs > 0.0 and np.isfinite(fs):
            best_final = best_final / fs

    print("returned sequence length=%d final-local-score=%.9f" %
          (best_final.size, quick_score(best_final)), flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in best_final]
```
